- **developmental-psychology**: Lifespan development from prenatal through aging. Covers Piaget's stages of cognitive development (sensorimotor, preoperational, concrete operational, formal operational), Erikson's psychosocial stages, attachment theory (Bowlby, Ainsworth, secure/avoidant/anxious/disorganized), moral development (Kohlberg, Gilligan), language acquisition (critical period, LAD, stages), adolescent identity formation, adult development, and aging. Use when analyzing how cognitive, social, emotional, and moral capacities develop and change across the human lifespan. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/psychology/developmental-psychology) · ★71 · 2 copies)

- **devex-reviewer**: Plan-mode developer experience review. Evaluates whether the planned tool, API, SDK, CLI, or platform will be a joy or a chore for developers to use. Rates on the 7 DX Characteristics (Usable, Credible, Findable, Useful, Valuable, Accessible, Desirable), measures time-to-Hello-World, and runs 8 review passes covering everything from install friction to migration paths. ([Chooch333/agent-library](https://github.com/Chooch333/agent-library/tree/HEAD/skills/devex-reviewer))
